# Enum: SubnationalJurisdiction

_Subnational or regional jurisdiction as an ISO 3166-2 subdivision code (e.g. US-CA for California, CA-QC for Quebec), from the DPV Location vocabulary (https://w3id.org/dpv/loc). Values are members of dpv:Region._

<div data-search-exclude markdown="1">

URI: [dpv:Region](https://w3id.org/dpv#Region)
